# trust v0.14.0

2026-11-16 · 9299 changes since v0.9.0

## Features

- **lean:** build source-bound carrier-gated Trust-IR libraries

- **certify:** define a kernel-checked finite scalar execution model
- **certify:** prove the complete UInt8 widening model family

- **trust2:** close and honestly measure six two-language audit gaps

- **thir-lower:** refuse the BODY that mints an unsignable Rust-ABI edge, not the whole crate

- **contracts:** admit a float parameter into contract scope (PARTIAL)

- **contracts:** a float magnitude bound now discharges its overflow obligation

- **diag:** TRUST\_BUNDLE\_FAMILY\_EXEMPT, and the full chain it measured
- **ref-self I1:** &self admission through a borrow-erased E6 parameter view — and three reviewers were right that it did not work end to end
- **crosscrate-proof I1:** cross-crate proof composition as a library with no caller — and both reviews were right that the design guarded one of three name→symbol sites
- **vcgen:** `min(a, c) <= c` as a global fact, and the unaimed sweep that found it
- **e6-view:** a frozen shared pointee skips the fields it cannot carry, and C2/K2 alone hold the line — measured
- **dyn:** C-SEALED — lattice rung 2 was wired to one call shape and not the other
- **ir-bridge:** admit a field projection on a SINGLE-PAYLOAD union
- **rung1:** use all three closed-world routes, not one of the three

- bind relaxed atomic lowering to a compiler-owned body

## Performance

- **vcgen:** the reaching-version oracle re-scanned everything, every time — 192-block generation 44 s -\> 11 s, byte-identical VCs
- **bridge:** lower once for the native completeness gate and the flip fallback
- **bridge:** compute the paired-condvar body digest lazily
- **bridge:** memoise the function-global scans behind the native own-block defs
- **bridge:** prune the guard walks instead of enumerating every CFG path
- **report:** elide the duplicate transport proof copy on published proved rows
- **verify:** memoise the bundled-member source digest by its exact bytes
- **extract:** pre-scan MIR for a certified Condvar::wait before the paired-condvar collector extracts anything
- **vcgen:** an uncached place name builds the function's local-name table once
- **once-check:** demand once-body facts in parallel
- **targo:** hash dependency paths before deterministic sorting
- **targo:** optimize release diagnostic hashing in dev builds
- **vcgen:** index expensive path-fact membership adaptively
- **vcgen:** skip unused normal outflow copies
- **vcgen:** borrow unchanged path-fact intersections
- **thir:** reuse replacement layout table within each check
- **thir:** index ordered struct registrations
- hash tracked source identity without temporary blob writes
- **verifier-api:** reuse artifact policy within immutable runs
- **bootstrap:** defer guarded intermediate Stage1 verifier tools
- coalesce pending drop-initialization worklist visits
- isolate bridge closure utility from solver dependencies
- bound per-file read allocations in source checks
- align bridge audit inputs and adopt checked Boolean branches
- **tippy:** avoid allocation for single import lookups
- **compiler:** port Span NonMaxU64 niche (#164291) and HIR trivial\_const + O(1) syntactic filter (#165112)
- **bridge:** stream checked immutable blobs through persistent Git
- **mir\_transform, trust-mir-extract:** skip redundant LazyLock MIR walks and optimize hot MIR queries
- **rustc\_mir\_transform:** lazy kernel-obligation universe and GlobalAsm pre-filter for CleanIsland walks
- **hir, mir-extract, verify:** deduplicate HIR map queries, avoid TypeWalker/freeze allocs, cache TRUST\_\* env flags
- **mir-transform:** avoid CFG cache invalidation and per-body allocations in MIR passes (PERF-MIR-PASS-CLEAN-REF-4)

## Refactors

- **coordination:** replace mandatory remote reservations with integration checks
- **tippy:** run trustc under its own name — no rustc sibling, no identity dance
- **dist, targo:** Trust ships its tools under Trust's names only
- **trust-verify:** OD-1 phase 2 — a cost-declined dependency callee discharges unknown, not failed
- **targo:** apply reviewed lint simplifications
- **targo:** name workflow inputs and scope callback borrows
- group compiler resource report inputs
- share local definition interpretation across consumers
- **wp:** share strict typed contract formula conversion
- **router:** share native claim validation with model checking
- **trust:** route incremental maintenance callers to native policy
- **trust:** retire Python incremental reclaimer after native migration
- **bootstrap:** document intentional source ABI normalization
- **targo:** route compiler SBOM output through checked writer boundary
- **targo:** align native module layout and unused APIs
- **targo:** move one append prefix through alias expansion
- **targo:** share bounded filesystem observations in Trust leaf
- **fs-observation:** share portable redirection predicate
- **router:** confine legacy MIR routing to test oracles
- **compiler:** own prepared dep-info and check final buffered flush
